# Reference photographs

The photographs FishDock's modelled places were built from. They were looked at, as 640-pixel previews fetched from Wikimedia Commons by the asset jobs on Aaron's PC (`downloads/commons-*` in FishDock-assets), to model buildings, piers and beaches from: none of them is shipped in the app or the repository, and no part of any of them is copied into a texture. Every one is under a licence that allows reuse with credit (CC0, public domain, CC BY or CC BY-SA), and each is credited here with its author and page all the same. Modelled from them: `src/lib/world3d/landmarksCromer.js`, `landmarksYarmouth.js`, `landmarksLowestoft.js` and the places' notes in `localDetail.js`.
